O001OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

The Code Nobody Reads

Addy Osmani · 2026-09-28

When human line-by-line reading no longer scales, independently justified checks and accountable approval must replace its assurance role.

Verification & reviewEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:027 Maker–Checker: Separate Production from AcceptanceInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:027 · Informs practice

    The proposed replacement for unscalable line-by-line reading informs how the checker obtains independently justified evidence and who owns approval. The essay's second-hand statistics do not validate a particular maker–checker arrangement.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPredictions and vendor/company statistics are reported second-hand; do not treat a risk-tier policy as externally validated.

Recorded summary. Osmani revises his earlier advice to inspect every generated line: routine low-risk changes may receive automated first-pass review, while high-blast-radius changes retain deeper scrutiny. He argues that review also transmitted knowledge and that engineering teams must retain both understanding and authority to refuse a release.

Reported findings / argument. Use explicit risk tiers and an evidence dossier rather than assuming every line is read; Keep independent tests and a named human acceptance authority; Protect mentoring, explainability and reviewer capacity.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Use explicit risk tiers and an evidence dossier rather than assuming every line is read; Keep independent tests and a named human acceptance authority; Protect mentoring, explainability and reviewer capacity.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O002OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Brownfield Agentic Engineering

Addy Osmani · 2026-09-14

Reliable agents in mature codebases need recovered tacit constraints and regression evidence before modifying code.

System knowledge & memoryEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:053 Legacy Code NavigationInforms practice · primary readingPAT:015 Regression Safety NetInforms practice
Why these practices (2)
  • PAT:053 · Informs practice

    Recovering tacit constraints before changing a mature repository is the navigation decision this practice addresses: distinguish existing behavior, intended obligations and missing system knowledge. The practitioner interpretation is not an evaluated navigation method.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:015 · Informs practice

    The explicit call for regression evidence informs which existing obligations must be protected before an agent modifies brownfield code. It does not establish that any particular regression suite covers all consequential behavior.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. The essay stresses that a repository rarely captures the full behavior of a legacy service: contracts, migrations, user journeys and institutional knowledge remain external. Small reversible changes, context gathering and reproducible tests are offered as controls against locally successful but architecturally wrong agent patches.

Reported findings / argument. Inventory external invariants and dependencies before delegation; Make brownfield changes incremental and reversible; Verify old behavior alongside new acceptance criteria.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Inventory external invariants and dependencies before delegation; Make brownfield changes incremental and reversible; Verify old behavior alongside new acceptance criteria.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O003OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Agentic Skill Decay

Addy Osmani · 2026-08-31

Developers must deliberately practice reasoning, debugging and code comprehension when agents perform most implementation work.

Skills & learningSystem knowledge & memory

Related practices

PAT:050 Learning Loop PreservationCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:050 · Cautions about practice

    The skill-decay argument identifies reasoning, debugging and comprehension as activities that must remain available when implementation is delegated. It motivates a learning-preservation boundary, without supplying a measured rate of developer skill loss.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani describes how automatic completion can remove the repetitions that once built expertise. He proposes predicting failures before asking an agent, explaining diffs, reconstructing decisions and periodically solving problems manually so proficiency does not disappear behind apparent task completion.

Reported findings / argument. Preserve manual practice and counterfactual reasoning exercises; Measure retained comprehension rather than prompt throughput; Avoid confusing agent task success with human competence.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Preserve manual practice and counterfactual reasoning exercises; Measure retained comprehension rather than prompt throughput; Avoid confusing agent task success with human competence.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O004OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Audit your Agent files

Addy Osmani · 2026-08-27

Agent configuration and memory are perishable operational assets that should be periodically revalidated.

System knowledge & memoryCode quality & security

Related practices

PAT:016 Structured Drift DetectionInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:016 · Informs practice

    Treating configuration and memory as perishable assets informs when retained agent instructions should be revalidated and stale versions excluded from current decisions. The article does not demonstrate an automated method for determining every semantic impact.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Instructions in AGENTS.md, CLAUDE.md, skills and durable memory can become outdated as tools and repositories change. Osmani recommends auditing usefulness, trimming redundant directives, testing outcomes and separating genuinely persistent facts from temporary guidance.

Reported findings / argument. Record an owner and expiry policy for agent instructions; Test whether each instruction changes verified behavior; Audit tool privileges and memory freshness independently.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Record an owner and expiry policy for agent instructions; Test whether each instruction changes verified behavior; Audit tool privileges and memory freshness independently.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O005Osmani

Human judgment doesn't leave the software factory. It relocates.

Addy Osmani · 2026-08-21

Agentic factories relocate human responsibility toward intent, quality criteria and authorization rather than eliminating it.

Engineering structureVerification & review

Related practices

PAT:034 Safety and Guardrails: Verification Is Not AuthorizationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:034 · Informs practice

    The relocation of human responsibility to intent, quality criteria and authorization informs the separation between permitted execution, technical checking and accountable acceptance. This is an organizational argument, not evidence that factories enforce those boundaries.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. The article compares human-supervised and largely unattended factories and asks who defines product intent, architectural trade-offs and unacceptable regressions. It advocates persistent checks, managed risk boundaries and explicit ownership even when many implementation steps run without a person present.

Reported findings / argument. Put human judgment at the intention and acceptance boundaries; Automate early regression and security checks; Escalate design ambiguity and irreversible operations to authorized owners.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Put human judgment at the intention and acceptance boundaries; Automate early regression and security checks; Escalate design ambiguity and irreversible operations to authorized owners.

O006OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Practical Loop Engineering

Addy Osmani · 2026-08-14

Effective autonomous loops begin with bounded goals, observable checks and explicit stopping rules.

Engineering structureWhat agents can do

Related practices

PAT:026 Run Until Done: Retry Against RealityInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:026 · Informs practice

    Bounded goals, observable checks and explicit stop conditions provide a concrete basis for deciding whether another autonomous attempt is warranted. The loop guidance does not establish that retries converge or that an agent may redefine success.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Drawing on concurrent agent use, Osmani separates tasks safe for fully bounded delegation from tasks that require close review. He describes iterative act-test-revise operation while emphasizing constraints, definition of done and final human judgment where risk is material.

Reported findings / argument. State an independent success predicate before granting retries; Limit cost, duration, permissions and edit scope; Escalate on ambiguity or verification exhaustion.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. State an independent success predicate before granting retries; Limit cost, duration, permissions and edit scope; Escalate on ambiguity or verification exhaustion.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O007Osmani

Agentic Code Quality

Addy Osmani · 2026-08-08

Quality assurance for generated code increasingly depends on repeatable harness checks, not only manual inspection or agent opinion.

Code quality & securityVerification & review

Related practices

PAT:027 Maker–Checker: Separate Production from AcceptanceInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:027 · Informs practice

    The preference for repeatable harness checks over agent opinion informs the checker’s evidence requirements for generated code. A practitioner recommendation for checks does not establish oracle independence or comprehensive quality assurance.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani notes the scalability limit of inspecting high-volume agent diffs and the instability of repeated model-review judgments. He favors consistent repository rules, static checks, tests and explicit quality gates surrounding generation, while keeping focused human scrutiny for risky changes.

Reported findings / argument. Move quality requirements into repeatable repository checks and agent harness gates; Track defects, architectural fit and post-merge maintenance—not merely model review approval; Retain risk-sensitive human review because multiple models can share error modes.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Move quality requirements into repeatable repository checks and agent harness gates; Track defects, architectural fit and post-merge maintenance—not merely model review approval; Retain risk-sensitive human review because multiple models can share error modes.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

No literal chapter citation recorded in the published edition.

O008Osmani

Software Factories, Light and Dark

Addy Osmani · 2026-07-22

A software factory is many governed loops; its level of human involvement changes both risk and total operating cost.

Engineering structureEconomics

Related practices

PAT:042 Capstone orchestraInforms practice · primary readingPAT:060 Cost ManagementInforms practice
Why these practices (2)
  • PAT:042 · Informs practice

    The factory taxonomy informs the decision to compose multiple governed loops only around a defined engineering outcome. Its conceptual operating models do not establish that a larger orchestration arrangement is effective.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:060 · Informs practice

    Changing the level of human involvement changes total operating cost, so the article informs including supervision and governance when comparing execution routes. Its projections are not observed comparative returns.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryA conceptual taxonomy and projections, not a controlled comparison of factory operating models.

Recorded summary. The essay contrasts "light" factories that retain human interpretation with "dark" factories that minimize review, exposing the trade-off between throughput and lost comprehension. Osmani argues that harness capability and measurable verification should determine safe autonomy, not the attraction of unattended execution.

Reported findings / argument. Separate an autonomous loop from the governance system supervising many loops; Measure cost of failures and rework, not just generation speed; Choose review intensity by reversibility and blast radius.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Separate an autonomous loop from the governance system supervising many loops; Measure cost of failures and rework, not just generation speed; Choose review intensity by reversibility and blast radius.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

Cited in published NDSE2 chapters

No literal chapter citation recorded in the published edition.

O009OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Own the Outer Loop

Addy Osmani · 2026-07-09

Engineers can delegate the inner execution loop while retaining ownership of quality criteria, verdicts and consequences.

Engineering structureVerification & review

Related practices

PAT:034 Safety and Guardrails: Verification Is Not AuthorizationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:034 · Informs practice

    Retaining ownership of quality criteria, verdicts and consequences while delegating execution directly informs the separation of execution from acceptance authority. The essay does not prove that any particular controller enforces those rights.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani distinguishes producing code and testing artifacts from accepting the evidence those checks produce. He locates professional accountability in the surrounding outer loop: intent, checks, risk appetite, provenance, approval and action if a released change fails.

Reported findings / argument. Define quality signals before execution; Separate the checker from the final acceptance authority; Require a named owner with authority to stop or roll back.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Define quality signals before execution; Separate the checker from the final acceptance authority; Require a named owner with authority to stop or roll back.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O010OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Agentic Autonomy Levels

Addy Osmani · 2026-07-03

Osmani separates autonomy into agency and orchestration axes, describing six operating levels from suggestions to management-by-exception.

What agents can doEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:034 Safety and Guardrails: Verification Is Not AuthorizationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:034 · Informs practice

    Separating agency from orchestration helps an owner specify which decisions are delegated at a chosen autonomy level. The taxonomy informs authorization design; its operating levels do not themselves grant permissions or validate safety.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani argues that a one-dimensional autonomy ladder hides the difference between what one agent may do and the ability to coordinate many agents. He proposes two axes—agency and orchestration—and six practical levels: assist, supervised action, scoped task delegation, goal-driven autonomy, parallel delegation, and managed-by-exception orchestration. Risk, reversibility, independent checks and escalation limit the appropriate level for any task.

Reported findings / argument. Grade individual-agent initiative separately from multi-agent orchestration; Use the six levels as a contextual operational taxonomy, not a validated maturity index; Set an explicit risk and verification ceiling for delegated work.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Grade individual-agent initiative separately from multi-agent orchestration; Use the six levels as a contextual operational taxonomy, not a validated maturity index; Set an explicit risk and verification ceiling for delegated work.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O011Osmani

Agentic Code Review

Addy Osmani · 2026-06-16

AI-generated code moves the bottleneck from writing to justified acceptance, making review strategy a core engineering skill.

Verification & reviewSkills & learning

Related practices

PAT:052 Review GauntletInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:052 · Informs practice

    The shift from writing to justified acceptance informs allocating review attention to the uncertainties introduced by generated changes. It is a practitioner account of a bottleneck, not a comparative evaluation of review routes.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani contrasts solo experiments and long-lived team repositories, arguing that risk, context, integration and shared learning demand different review methods. Review must consider correctness evidence, comprehension and how design decisions survive the handoff.

Reported findings / argument. Allocate human attention by risk rather than diff size; Audit automated reviewer false positives and misses; Preserve review as a team knowledge-transfer mechanism.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Allocate human attention by risk rather than diff size; Audit automated reviewer false positives and misses; Preserve review as a team knowledge-transfer mechanism.

O012OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Loop Engineering

Addy Osmani · 2026-06-08

Osmani identifies five interoperable loop-building primitives—scheduling, worktrees, skills, connectors and subagents—plus durable external memory.

Engineering structureWhat agents can do

Related practices

PAT:042 Capstone orchestraInforms practice · primary readingPAT:031 Worktrees: Isolate the Write DomainsInforms practice
Why these practices (2)
  • PAT:042 · Informs practice

    The enumerated loop primitives inform choosing which mechanisms a composed agent workflow actually needs, including durable memory. The pattern description does not show that installing every primitive improves reliability.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:031 · Informs practice

    Worktrees are an explicit primitive in the loop account, making the article relevant to separating concurrent write domains before integration. This thematic association does not establish that the described loops isolate shared services or logical ownership.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryA practitioner engineering pattern; the article does not establish comparative loop reliability by controlled experiment.

Recorded summary. Loop engineering means configuring a system that discovers work, dispatches agents, checks outputs, records progress and repeats rather than manually prompting each step. Osmani identifies five recurring primitives: automations/scheduling, isolated Git worktrees, reusable skills, plugins and connectors, and specialized subagents. A separate persistent memory or task board is the sixth requirement for continuity. He also warns about verification, comprehension debt and unpredictable token costs.

Reported findings / argument. Schedule discovery and triage but bound side effects; Isolate parallel work in worktrees; share intentions via tested skills/connectors and durable state; Separate the maker from the checker, measure token usage, and escalate when independent checks fail.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Schedule discovery and triage but bound side effects; Isolate parallel work in worktrees; share intentions via tested skills/connectors and durable state; Separate the maker from the checker, measure token usage, and escalate when independent checks fail.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O013OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

Long-running Agents

Addy Osmani · 2026-04-30

Agents operating across sessions require durable task state, resumability, recovery and verifiable progress.

System knowledge & memoryWhat agents can do

Related practices

PAT:020 File-System as State MachineInforms practice · primary readingPAT:072 Handoff ChainInforms practice
Why these practices (2)
  • PAT:020 · Informs practice

    Durable task state, resumability and recovery inform representing progress through inspectable state transitions rather than conversational completion claims. The summary does not establish transactional storage or tested recovery guarantees.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:072 · Informs practice

    Cross-session operation and verifiable progress inform the state a successor needs at handoff, including unfinished work and recovery information. Resumability is a requirement here, not an independently evaluated handoff result.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. The article examines context-window exhaustion, repeated mistakes and premature "done" claims in workflows lasting days. It recommends structured progress artifacts, checkpoints, clean workspaces and independent acceptance tests to support handoffs between agent sessions.

Reported findings / argument. Persist decisions and current state outside the transient model context; Make retries idempotent and restartable; Verify continuity and completion against external artifacts.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Persist decisions and current state outside the transient model context; Make retries idempotent and restartable; Verify continuity and completion against external artifacts.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

O014Osmani

The Agent Stack Bet

Addy Osmani · 2026-04-18

Production agents need identity, authorization, observability and recovery controls beyond an impressive base model.

Code quality & securityEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:034 Safety and Guardrails: Verification Is Not AuthorizationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:034 · Informs practice

    The requirement for identity and authorization beyond model capability informs separating permission enforcement from model-generated actions and success claims. The architectural argument does not certify a production agent's controls.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani describes fragile production plumbing: broad service-account permissions, schema drift, opaque state and broken recovery. The remedy is an explicit agent stack with task-scoped access, traceability, policy enforcement and durable error handling.

Reported findings / argument. Bind every action to scoped identity and human ownership; Restrict tool permissions and monitor effects; Design recoverable operations and audit trails for runtime failures.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Bind every action to scoped identity and human ownership; Restrict tool permissions and monitor effects; Design recoverable operations and audit trails for runtime failures.

O015Osmani

Is the IDE dead?

Addy Osmani · 2026-03-20

Asynchronous agent orchestration may become the primary developer control surface, while editors remain tools for targeted inspection.

Engineering structureWhat agents can do

Related practices

PAT:025 The Editor-Native Manual LoopInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:025 · Informs practice

    The retained role of editors for targeted inspection informs choosing a human-guided session when a change needs close judgment, even if orchestration becomes asynchronous. This is a prospective control-surface argument, not a measured advantage of manual loops.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani discusses workbench and control-plane interfaces for coordinating cloud and local coding agents. The claim is about a shift in where engineers plan, inspect and approve work, not proof that code editors will disappear.

Reported findings / argument. Teach delegation and inspection as separate activities; Compare orchestration tools on observable state and control; Treat claims about the IDE dying as forecasts.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Teach delegation and inspection as separate activities; Compare orchestration tools on observable state and control; Treat claims about the IDE dying as forecasts.

14 More lessons from 14 years at Google

Addy Osmani · 2026-02-12

Long-lived engineering teams depend on trust, clear decision-making, effective communication and organizational stewardship.

Engineering structureSkills & learning

Related practices

PAT:072 Handoff ChainInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:072 · Informs practice

    Clear decision-making, communication and long-term stewardship inform what an engineering handoff should preserve for its successor. This is a thematic use of a career retrospective, not evidence that the essay describes or validates an agent handoff protocol.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPersonal career retrospective; no original AI-specific field experiment.

Recorded summary. A second retrospective about long-term team effectiveness stresses the structures around projects: communication, ownership, priorities, collaboration and shared decisions. These lessons provide organizational context rather than new research on AI agents.

Reported findings / argument. Make decision rights and handoffs visible; Value team reliability above heroic individual throughput; Use as background principles, not an AI impact study.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Make decision rights and handoffs visible; Value team reliability above heroic individual throughput; Use as background principles, not an AI impact study.

O017OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

The 80% Problem in Agentic Coding

Addy Osmani · 2026-01-28

When agents implement most code, developers can accumulate comprehension debt even while shipping more changes.

Skills & learningSystem knowledge & memory

Related practices

PAT:048 The Explainability TestCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:048 · Cautions about practice

    Comprehension debt despite increased shipping makes reconstruction of behavior and failure modes a separate acceptance concern. The illustrative 80% framing cannot quantify the prevalence of weak developer understanding.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryThe 80% figure expresses an illustrative workflow shift; it is not a population-level experimental estimate.

Recorded summary. Extending his earlier "70% problem", Osmani frames agent-heavy development as an inversion between authorship and understanding. Speed moves the bottleneck toward explaining decisions, tracing behavior and maintaining the code after the generation session ends.

Reported findings / argument. Require explain-back, behavioral traces and repair exercises; Measure whether an engineer can modify agent-written code later; Treat the 80% phrase as illustration, not a measured universal ratio.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Require explain-back, behavioral traces and repair exercises; Measure whether an engineer can modify agent-written code later; Treat the 80% phrase as illustration, not a measured universal ratio.

Provenance
Osmani public Substack sitemaps 2024/2025/2026 plus individual source URLs; independent editorial review 2026-10-10

Cited in published NDSE2 chapters

O018OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

How to write a good spec for AI agents

Addy Osmani · 2026-01-19

A useful agent specification is compact, testable and iterative rather than a giant prompt resembling an unvalidated requirements dump.

Engineering structureSystem knowledge & memory

Related practices

PAT:043 Spec-First GenerationInforms practice · primary readingPAT:064 Specification Scale ManagementInforms practicePAT:068 Progressive SpecificationInforms practice
Why these practices (3)
  • PAT:043 · Informs practice

    A compact, testable specification informs making the acceptance question explicit before generation. The guidance does not establish that concise specifications are complete or that implementation can authorize missing requirements.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:064 · Informs practice

    The warning against a giant unvalidated requirements dump informs keeping specifications compact and governable as work grows. Its iterative guidance does not establish complete dependency tracking or a universal optimum for document size.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:068 · Informs practice

    The recommendation for iterative specifications informs proceeding on settled behavior while keeping unresolved questions explicit. It does not justify repeated document rewriting as a substitute for authorized decisions.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani provides guidance on the degree of detail in requirements, decomposition into small tasks, read-only planning and successive execution. Intent, constraints, expected behavior and testing guidance become checkpoints throughout development.

Reported findings / argument. Specify desired behavior and forbidden regressions separately from implementation detail; Plan before writes, then build in small independently checked increments; Update specification when evidence reveals an ambiguity.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Specify desired behavior and forbidden regressions separately from implementation detail; Plan before writes, then build in small independently checked increments; Update specification when evidence reveals an ambiguity.

O019Osmani

Code Review in the Age of AI

Addy Osmani · 2026-01-05

The rise in generated changes makes code review an explicit burden-of-proof and accountability process.

Verification & reviewEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:052 Review GauntletInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:052 · Informs practice

    The article frames review as an accountable burden-of-proof process, informing which checks and decision owners are needed for a generated change. It supplies a review argument rather than measured evidence that more review stages improve outcomes.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. The essay argues that tests, reproducible manual checks and a stated risk assessment should accompany every proposal. Teams should preserve contextual knowledge and ownership rather than confusing a model-generated diff with evidence that the resulting change is safe.

Reported findings / argument. Attach objective verification artifacts to pull requests; Review intent, integration risk and sensitive paths; Never treat a passing self-generated test as independent approval.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Attach objective verification artifacts to pull requests; Review intent, integration risk and sensitive paths; Never treat a passing self-generated test as independent approval.

How Good Is AI at Coding React (Really)?

Addy Osmani · 2025-12-29

React-related AI success is highly task-dependent, with integration and usability much harder to establish than component scaffolding.

What agents can doVerification & review

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Cautions about practice

    The contrast between easy React scaffolding and harder integration or usability cautions against promoting a successful prototype directly into maintained software. The synthesized benchmarks use different tasks and cannot establish a universal adoption threshold.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryNumerical benchmark claims are a synthesis of separate benchmarks with different tasks, versions and measurement methods; not directly comparable.

Recorded summary. Using third-party web and coding benchmarks, Osmani contrasts relatively tractable isolated UI tasks with weaker multi-step integration. He recommends context-rich specifications, UI standards, accessibility checks and browser-level evaluation instead of equating code generation with product quality.

Reported findings / argument. Benchmark scores have task- and version-specific denominators; Use browser tests and accessibility review for end-to-end UI changes; Treat reported percentages as third-party snapshot results, not the author’s experimental measurement.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Benchmark scores have task- and version-specific denominators; Use browser tests and accessibility review for end-to-end UI changes; Treat reported percentages as third-party snapshot results, not the author’s experimental measurement.

O021Osmani

My LLM coding workflow going into 2026

Addy Osmani · 2025-12-18

Planning, scoped delegation and continuous checking outperform an unmanaged prompt-to-merge workflow as an engineering practice.

Engineering structureVerification & review

Related practices

PAT:001 Plan-Then-ExecuteInforms practice · primary readingPAT:046 Small Verifiable ChunksInforms practice
Why these practices (2)
  • PAT:001 · Informs practice

    Planning before scoped delegation informs separating the approved result from an execution plan that is revised as checks produce observations. The workflow preference remains practitioner guidance, not a controlled claim of outperforming alternatives.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:046 · Informs practice

    Scoped delegation and continuous checking inform choosing increments small enough to verify a coherent change. The article does not establish a universal chunk size or prove that every split reduces integration work.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani describes his own evolving approach to working with coding assistants: articulate a plan, provide repository context, implement bounded steps, execute tests and remain accountable for reviews and integration.

Reported findings / argument. Start from a task brief and acceptance criteria; Review after each small increment; Distinguish personal workflow preference from measured performance gains.

Treat AI-Generated code as a draft

Addy Osmani · 2025-11-25

AI-generated code should be treated as an untrusted draft and not merged without responsible understanding and verification.

Verification & reviewSystem knowledge & memory

Related practices

PAT:027 Maker–Checker: Separate Production from AcceptanceInforms practice · primary readingPAT:048 The Explainability TestInforms practice
Why these practices (2)
  • PAT:027 · Informs practice

    Treating generated code as an untrusted draft informs withholding acceptance until responsible verification exists. The author's later qualification of the prescription should remain visible; the draft metaphor does not itself supply an independent oracle.

    Explore this practice with its sources →
  • PAT:048 · Informs practice

    Responsible understanding before merge informs asking the maintainer to explain the candidate's behavior and potential failures. An explanation is a comprehension check, not proof that the generated implementation is correct.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPrescriptive practice subsequently qualified by the same author in September 2026.

Recorded summary. This earlier essay stresses reading AI code closely so that humans connect implementation back to intent, testing and maintenance. Its strong line-by-line reading prescription is explicitly revised in the September 2026 "The Code Nobody Reads" essay, which advocates risk-tiered independent checking.

Reported findings / argument. Verify AI output against requirements before acceptance; Record decision ownership; Document the change in the author’s proposed review policy over time.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Verify AI output against requirements before acceptance; Record decision ownership; Document the change in the author’s proposed review policy over time.

Critical Thinking during the age of AI

Addy Osmani · 2025-11-21

Critical questions about purpose, assumptions, context and evidence remain necessary when AI supplies plausible solutions.

Skills & learningEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:074 Anti-Sycophancy EnforcementInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:074 · Informs practice

    Questions about purpose, assumptions, context and evidence inform a review task that challenges plausible AI solutions with specific objections. Critical questioning is guidance here, not evidence that a skeptical prompt removes shared blind spots.

O025Osmani

Conductors to Orchestrators: The Future of Agentic Coding

Addy Osmani · 2025-11-01

Developers are moving from guiding one agent toward coordinating multiple asynchronously executing agents.

Engineering structureJobs & roles

Related practices

PAT:056 Parallel Work OrchestrationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:056 · Informs practice

    The move from one guided agent to multiple asynchronous agents informs planning coordination and integration responsibility across concurrent work. Role forecasts and examples do not establish higher accepted throughput or future hiring outcomes.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryRole forecasts and workflow examples, not validated hiring or productivity outcomes.

Recorded summary. The essay distinguishes a single-agent "conductor" role from an "orchestrator" that allocates work among parallel agents with isolated workspaces. It forecasts changes in the engineer’s day-to-day role while acknowledging tool maturity and coordination costs.

Reported findings / argument. Isolate concurrent branches and agent responsibilities; Assign merge and conflict-resolution ownership; Treat role changes as a scenario to evaluate, not an established labor-market effect.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Isolate concurrent branches and agent responsibilities; Assign merge and conflict-resolution ownership; Treat role changes as a scenario to evaluate, not an established labor-market effect.

Vibe coding is not the same as AI-Assisted engineering.

Addy Osmani · 2025-08-30

Fast conversational prototyping and accountable AI-assisted engineering are distinct development regimes.

Engineering structureCode quality & security

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Cautions about practice

    The distinction between conversational prototyping and accountable engineering informs keeping the experiment's adoption decision separate from its apparent success. The practitioner distinction does not establish a measured boundary between safe and unsafe prototypes.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani distinguishes exploratory prompting from production practices with design documents, test-driven changes, review and ownership. He argues that treating either workflow as universally appropriate obscures the requirements of maintenance-intensive software.

Reported findings / argument. Choose rigor appropriate to the target environment; Require explicit acceptance contracts for production work; Teach the transition from prototype to maintainable system.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Choose rigor appropriate to the target environment; Require explicit acceptance contracts for production work; Teach the transition from prototype to maintainable system.

O029OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

The reality of AI-Assisted software engineering productivity

Addy Osmani · 2025-08-16

Perceived personal acceleration with coding AI does not necessarily translate into net team or release-level productivity.

ProductivityEconomics

Related practices

PAT:059 Measurement & LearningCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:059 · Cautions about practice

    The mismatch between perceived personal acceleration and team or release outcomes cautions against using individual speed as the productivity endpoint. Heterogeneous third-party percentages cannot be pooled into a causal workflow gain.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryThe percentages reported by Osmani come from diverse third-party surveys and experiments; no single pooled causal estimate is supplied.

Coding for the Future Agentic World

Addy Osmani · 2025-07-29

CLI agents and background task runners extend software generation into tool-using, multi-step workflows, but require more oversight.

What agents can doEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:003 Background Agent CIInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:003 · Informs practice

    Background task runners and multi-step tool use inform defining what unattended work may prepare and what oversight remains outside that job. The article does not establish release readiness from background task completion.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani surveys emerging agentic tools and contrasts autocomplete, interactive assistants and agents that edit files, run commands, debug and propose pull requests. He argues that more capable execution increases the value of planning, context and final approval.

Reported findings / argument. Teach capabilities with bounded task examples; Evaluate tool use and integration separately from model fluency; Retain explicit sandbox and final release authority.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Teach capabilities with bounded task examples; Evaluate tool use and integration separately from model fluency; Retain explicit sandbox and final release authority.

Context Engineering: Bringing Engineering Discipline to Prompts

Addy Osmani · 2025-07-13

Reliable prompts are assembled from selected system context, tools and constraints rather than crafted by wording alone.

System knowledge & memoryEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:047 Context EngineeringInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:047 · Informs practice

    The emphasis on selected system context, tools and constraints informs assembling task context by role and relevance rather than prompt wording alone. It does not establish that additional context guarantees system understanding.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. The essay develops context engineering as information architecture: relevant files, architectural records, error logs, schemas, examples and acceptance constraints. It emphasizes curating high-signal context so a coding agent can act within repository realities.

Reported findings / argument. Treat context as a versioned engineering input; Prefer relevant artifacts over exhaustive dumps; Verify provenance and freshness of retrieved context.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Treat context as a versioned engineering input; Prefer relevant artifacts over exhaustive dumps; Verify provenance and freshness of retrieved context.

The AI-Native Software Engineer

Addy Osmani · 2025-07-01

An AI-native engineer integrates assistance into ordinary work while retaining domain expertise and continuous learning.

Skills & learningEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:012 AI-Accelerated Learning and Skill DevelopmentInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:012 · Informs practice

    Integrating assistance with retained domain expertise and continuous learning informs choosing learning objectives alongside assisted delivery. Hypothetical productivity multipliers do not demonstrate independent skill acquisition or transfer.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryProspective productivity multipliers are hypothetical and should not be cited as empirical measurements.

Recorded summary. Osmani presents a practical skills playbook for pairing with coding assistants, delegating repetitive work and learning better tool use. The article is intentionally aspirational about possible productivity multiples rather than a controlled performance study.

Reported findings / argument. Teach effective delegation alongside manual competence; Create deliberate skill-updating routines; Do not present advertised 2x–10x multipliers as validated general results.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Teach effective delegation alongside manual competence; Create deliberate skill-updating routines; Do not present advertised 2x–10x multipliers as validated general results.

O033Osmani

The "Trust, But Verify" Pattern For AI-Assisted Engineering

Addy Osmani · 2025-06-09

Verification becomes the bottleneck when generation is cheap; every AI-produced change needs a justified check.

Verification & reviewEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:027 Maker–Checker: Separate Production from AcceptanceInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:027 · Informs practice

    Requiring a justified check for each AI-produced change informs the evidence the checker must obtain before acceptance. The practitioner argument does not make a generated check independent merely because it exists.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani frames the assistant as a capable but fallible collaborator whose output must be tested and reviewed before adoption. The post translates automation-bias concerns into reviewer practices, guardrails and accountable sign-off.

Reported findings / argument. Check against independent requirements and regression oracles; Record the outcome of manual and automated review; Teach why plausible code is not proof of correctness.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Check against independent requirements and regression oracles; Record the outcome of manual and automated review; Teach why plausible code is not proof of correctness.

O034Osmani

The Prompt Engineering Playbook for Programmers

Addy Osmani · 2025-05-27

Effective developer prompts state a bounded problem, relevant context, constraints and the expected verification method.

Engineering structureSystem knowledge & memory

Related practices

PAT:043 Spec-First GenerationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:043 · Informs practice

    A bounded problem, constraints and an expected verification method inform stating the behavior to be accepted before asking for code. The prompt playbook does not establish that a well-formed request captures every stakeholder obligation.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Through contrasting examples, Osmani demonstrates how explicit role, environment, scope, format and feedback loops can improve coding-assistant usefulness. The book can reuse the patterns as task-envelope teaching devices rather than treat prompt form as a substitute for formal specifications.

Reported findings / argument. Provide concrete files and constraints with each request; Make successful output observable; Iterate with feedback instead of claiming prompt wording ensures reliability.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Provide concrete files and constraints with each request; Make successful output observable; Iterate with feedback instead of claiming prompt wording ensures reliability.

Addy Osmani · 2025-05-10

Removing junior engineering positions can damage apprenticeship and the future pool of experienced maintainers.

Jobs & rolesSkills & learning

Related practices

PAT:050 Learning Loop PreservationInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:050 · Informs practice

    The apprenticeship argument informs preserving opportunities for junior engineers to build the reasoning needed for later maintenance. It is hiring and career guidance, not causal evidence about employment trends or measured learning effects.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryA prescriptive career and hiring essay; no original causal analysis of employment trends.

Recorded summary. Osmani argues that early-career roles must evolve toward debugging, system comprehension, collaboration and verification as assistants automate routine output. This is a workforce-development argument, not a causal demonstration that AI has or has not changed entry-level hiring.

Reported findings / argument. Protect supervised learning opportunities for junior developers; Evaluate apprenticeship capacity alongside coding throughput; Compare hiring claims with primary labor-market studies.

O036Osmani

Avoiding Skill Atrophy in the Age of AI

Addy Osmani · 2025-04-25

Using AI to avoid every difficult step risks eroding the reasoning skills needed to supervise future AI output.

Skills & learningSystem knowledge & memory

Related practices

PAT:050 Learning Loop PreservationCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:050 · Cautions about practice

    Avoiding every difficult step removes reasoning practice needed to supervise later output, directly identifying a boundary for learning-preserving assistance. The interpretation does not quantify retained-skill loss.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani draws an analogy with other forms of cognitive offloading and recommends intentional debugging, independent implementation, explanation and review. The essay identifies a plausible risk, while the size and persistence of skill loss require empirical evaluation.

Reported findings / argument. Maintain opportunities for unaided practice; Use delayed comprehension and transfer tests; Keep the analogy separate from evidence of measured developer skill decay.

Editorial use. Maintain opportunities for unaided practice; Use delayed comprehension and transfer tests; Keep the analogy separate from evidence of measured developer skill decay.

O037Osmani

Vibe Coding is not an excuse for low-quality work

Addy Osmani · 2025-04-18

Rapid AI prototyping does not exempt production changes from ordinary software quality obligations.

Code quality & securityEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Cautions about practice

    The persistence of ordinary quality obligations cautions against treating rapid prototype generation as approval for production use. This is prescriptive guidance, not a validated readiness checklist.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani warns that easy generation can conceal bugs, weak design and unmaintainable dependencies. He urges standard review, architectural judgment, tests and security checks even when non-specialists can produce working prototypes.

Reported findings / argument. Evaluate generated artifacts against system standards; Require a maintenance plan for promoted prototypes; Measure longer-run defects and complexity, not demo velocity.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Evaluate generated artifacts against system standards; Require a maintenance plan for promoted prototypes; Measure longer-run defects and complexity, not demo velocity.

What are AI Agents? why do they matter?

Addy Osmani · 2025-04-11

An agent differs from one-shot model completion by using tools and feedback to pursue a goal over multiple steps.

What agents can doEngineering structure

Related practices

PAT:026 Run Until Done: Retry Against RealityInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:026 · Informs practice

    The distinction between one-shot completion and tool/feedback-driven goal pursuit informs making each repeated attempt observable and bounded. The general agent explanation does not establish convergence or a tested stopping controller.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. The post offers an introductory description of agent perception, planning, tool use, action and iteration. It is useful for defining system boundaries and distinguishing model capability from the reliability of a surrounding task workflow.

Reported findings / argument. Model agents as bounded goal-directed processes; Separate tool authority from language-model output; Require observable success and failure states.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Model agents as bounded goal-directed processes; Separate tool authority from language-model output; Require observable success and failure states.

Vibe Coding: Revolution or Reckless Abandon?

Addy Osmani · 2025-04-03

Vibe coding can democratize prototypes while amplifying unnoticed security and maintainability risks.

Code quality & securitySkills & learning

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Cautions about practice

    The combination of accessible prototyping and unnoticed security or maintainability risks informs a separate adoption review after exploration. The essay supplies a risk argument, not measured rates of prototype failure.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. Osmani discusses enthusiastic prompting with minimal code inspection and contrasts its accessibility with risks from incorrect assumptions, latent defects and insufficient operator understanding. The essay calls for professional checks as project consequence increases.

Reported findings / argument. Separate creative prototyping from production acceptance; Add security and regression checks early; Train users to recognize the limits of superficial functional demos.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Separate creative prototyping from production acceptance; Add security and regression checks early; Train users to recognize the limits of superficial functional demos.

MCP: What It Is and Why It Matters

Addy Osmani · 2025-03-27

The Model Context Protocol standardizes agent access to external resources and tools, increasing integration reach and the importance of permission controls.

What agents can doCode quality & security

Related practices

PAT:030 Connectors and Tools: Move Exact Work Outside the WeightsInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:030 · Informs practice

    Standardized access to resources and tools informs selecting inspectable connectors for precise operations. Broader integration reach still requires permission controls; protocol use does not establish that an operation is authorized.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryPractitioner interpretation; not primary experimental evidence.

Recorded summary. An accessible explanation of MCP clients, servers and tool interoperability, including opportunities for richer context. For the book it illustrates the distinction between a convenient integration protocol and the authorization, sandbox and trust decisions the protocol cannot solve alone.

Reported findings / argument. Teach interface contracts separately from access policy; Review external tool descriptions as untrusted input; Constrain secrets, scopes and side-effecting calls.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Teach interface contracts separately from access policy; Review external tool descriptions as untrusted input; Constrain secrets, scopes and side-effecting calls.

  • PAT:057 · Informs practice

Beyond the 70%: Maximizing the human 30% of AI-assisted coding

Addy Osmani · 2025-03-13

The difficult last-mile work in AI-assisted development remains architecture, requirements, integration and verification.

Skills & learningProductivity

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Cautions about practice

    Architecture, requirements, integration and verification remain after easy generation, so the article cautions against using a mostly working prototype as a completion claim. Its 70/30 metaphor is not a measured engineering-effort allocation.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryThe 70/30 split is a practitioner metaphor and is not independently measured in this essay.

Recorded summary. Expanding his 2024 "70% problem" framing, Osmani discusses the durable value of engineers who can interpret behavior, handle edge cases and maintain systems. The percentages are rhetorical heuristics for relative task difficulty, not tested ratios of project completion.

Reported findings / argument. Distinguish generated scaffold from production-ready change; Teach system-level debugging and design; Avoid quoting 70/30 as a measured software engineering constant.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Distinguish generated scaffold from production-ready change; Teach system-level debugging and design; Avoid quoting 70/30 as a measured software engineering constant.

Applied "Software Engineering at Google"

Addy Osmani · 2025-03-06

Testing, review, documentation and sustainable ownership are established software-engineering practices that remain useful with AI-generated code.

Engineering structureVerification & review

Related practices

PAT:015 Regression Safety NetInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:015 · Informs practice

    The testing and sustainable-ownership synthesis informs retaining checks for existing obligations when generated changes are introduced. It draws on an established book rather than providing new evidence of an AI-specific regression benefit.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryExpository synthesis of an existing software engineering book, not original AI research.

Reported findings / argument. Reuse established tests-and-change reasoning; Distinguish well-founded engineering precedent from measured agent performance; Use as contextual support for governance.

O044Osmani

Speech-to-Code: Vibe Coding with Voice

Addy Osmani · 2025-02-28

Voice input changes how developers express tasks to coding assistants but does not remove the need to verify resulting software.

What agents can doProductivity

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingIllustrates practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Illustrates practice

    The first-person voice-to-code demonstration illustrates another way to express and explore a candidate application. Changing the input modality does not remove verification requirements, and the demonstration supplies no comparative productivity measurement.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryFirst-person tool demonstration without measured comparative productivity.

Recorded summary. Osmani demonstrates dictating instructions into an AI coding workflow using speech tools and describes personal ergonomic and velocity benefits. The improvement is experiential; no controlled comparison of input methods or final code quality is provided.

Reported findings / argument. Teach modality-independent task specification; Evaluate accessibility and ergonomics separately from correctness; Do not extrapolate a personal speedup to all teams.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Teach modality-independent task specification; Evaluate accessibility and ergonomics separately from correctness; Do not extrapolate a personal speedup to all teams.

O045Osmani

Personal Software: The Unbundling of the Programmer?

Addy Osmani · 2025-02-09

Lower creation costs may expand demand for one-off and personal applications, an economic hypothesis distinct from measured productivity or employment effects.

EconomicsJobs & roles

Related practices

PAT:060 Cost ManagementInforms practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:060 · Informs practice

    The personal-software scenario informs considering how lower creation cost changes which small applications might be worth building. It does not measure full maintenance cost, adoption, welfare or employment effects, so those remain separate cost-accounting questions.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryForward-looking economic scenario; no primary adoption or welfare evaluation.

Recorded summary. Osmani explores a world in which individuals and domain specialists build small personalized applications too narrow to justify traditional development contracts. The claim is about enabling software that previously would not be built at all. It is a forward-looking long-tail demand scenario, not a measured labor substitution, return-on-investment or full lifecycle cost study.

Reported findings / argument. Separate prototype creation cost from lifecycle cost; Evaluate new demand and substitution scenarios independently; Mark market forecasts as hypotheses.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Separate prototype creation cost from lifecycle cost; Evaluate new demand and substitution scenarios independently; Mark market forecasts as hypotheses.

O049OsmaniIn the published NDSE2 bibliography

The 70% problem: Hard truths about AI-assisted coding

Addy Osmani · 2024-12-04

Getting a prototype mostly implemented with AI is far easier than completing the remaining integrations and engineering assurances.

ProductivityCode quality & security

Related practices

PAT:045 Exploratory Vibe CodingCautions about practice · primary reading
Why these practices (1)
  • PAT:045 · Cautions about practice

    The gap between a mostly implemented prototype and completed integrations cautions against treating visible functionality as engineering completion. The anecdotal 70% figure cannot be used as a quantitative adoption gate.

    Explore this practice with its sources →

Primary reading is the main classification for this record, not a strength-of-evidence score. Associations use retained Atlas annotations; they add no manuscript citation or claim that the complete practice was validated.

Evidence, context & provenance
Evidence boundaryThe 70% estimate is an anecdotal framing, not a validated quantitative benchmark.

Recorded summary. Osmani contrasts fast "zero-to-MVP" generation with difficult iteration, debugging and production readiness in realistic codebases. His memorable 70% phrasing is descriptive and experiential, not a controlled measurement of task success.

Reported findings / argument. Measure end-to-end shipping and rework instead of scaffolding speed; Identify integration and maintenance as separate work; Preserve the heuristic character of the percentage.

Source type, as recorded: Practitioner essay / technical guidance (not peer-reviewed)

Editorial use. Measure end-to-end shipping and rework instead of scaffolding speed; Identify integration and maintenance as separate work; Preserve the heuristic character of the percentage.
